Fonte: design/tripo/oraculo-roteiro-tripo.md

Oráculo: roteiro Tripo do piloto ao nível 150 (copia-e-cola)

Piloto APROVADO pelo criador (19/11), alinhado antes do Canvas 002. O prompt 1a é a referência: sacerdote-guerreiro vidente, alto e atlético (nunca frágil, monge velho ou eremita), postura de comando, olhar penetrante, cabelo escuro curto e barba aparada; diadema fino de ouro velho com cristal azul-claro (cresce até tiara); manto de batalha marfim de gola alta aberto em painéis sobre calça escura e botas altas (nunca roupão, kimono ou sandália); capelete, faixa e estola azul-noite com sinos e olhos bordados em ouro; braçadeiras de couro; livro das profecias acorrentado no quadril; bolsa de cristais; varinha clara com cristal.
- Mulher: corte feminino real, cabelo escuro em coque alto sob o diadema (nada caindo nos ombros); todo prompt de MULHER termina com "Keep her feminine cut.".
- Bloco ORACLE IDENTITY reescrito para o piloto (Forbidden: roupão/kimono, sandália, monge/eremita, cabelo solto nos ombros, corpo frágil, papa/cardeal, brilho exceto no 150, cartum).
- Fundo padrão em todo HOMEM/MULHER: cinza neutro liso #7A7A7A de estúdio, luz frontal suave (sem pergaminho, chão, cenário ou vinheta).
- Nível 1 = a roupa do piloto. Braços livres para variar ao longo da evolução, sempre mais fortes, nunca mais leves. A "alça vazia" virou o livro acorrentado.
Status: pronto para colar, 19/11 (padronização final: níveis 1 a 37 completos; 41 a 150 em modelo de ramos). Nada foi gerado. Autor: Claude (art-director). Modelo: design/tripo/barbaro-cadeia-armaduras-1-150.md (mesmos níveis, chaves e intermediárias, modelo de ramos do 41 ao 150: ITEM neutro, HOMEM e MULHER por ramo, ARMA única).
Fontes: design/art/visual-equipamento-por-classe.md (§3, §4, §10, §11), design/gdd/class-tree-v2.md §1.7, design/narrative/historias-das-classes-v2.md §4 (Terraço do Pavio, sinos e presos). Entrada só de imagens nossas (nunca Asset Store nem Fab).
Ramos: A = Sineiro (40) → Mestre do Badalo (60) → Badalo de Prata (80) → Sino-Mestre (100) → Sino Vivo (130): sinos e toques. B = Profeta (40) → Voz dos Presos (60) → Intérprete de Almas (80) → Vidente do Veio (100) → Voz Una (130): olhos e previsão.

0. Resumo (5 linhas)

  1. Identidade: a voz que escuta quem ninguém mais escuta (nunca padre, freira ou mago branco); sacerdote-guerreiro vidente (piloto aprovado 19/11), sem metal pesado; manto de batalha marfim de gola alta aberto em painéis sobre calça escura e botas altas, estola azul-noite com sinos e olhos bordados em ouro, livro das profecias acorrentado no quadril e bolsa de cristais; capelete de ombros desde a S2, capa longa cobrindo tudo só do 41 (estilo Diablo IV).
  2. Cor dominante: marfim #EDE3C8 (45-55%); azul-noite #2A3550 em blocos grandes (25-35%): estola, faixa, sub-roupão, forros, punhos; ouro claro #D9B45A (10-15%: olho, borda, sinos). O azul tem de se ler de longe contra o marfim: nunca borrão branco, bege ou cinza; nunca preto dominante.
  3. Silhueta-assinatura: sino alto: roupão que abre do quadril num barrado largo, estola vertical azul cortando o marfim, tiara subindo acima da cabeça e (do 41) capa em sino; do 60, moldura-troféu nas costas; simétrica.
  4. Peça de cabeça fixa: diadema fino de ouro velho com cristal azul-claro no 1 (cresce até tiara/coroa com pontas, sinos ou olhos até o 150); sem véu, cabelo sempre visível (mulher em coque alto).
  5. Arma: varinha + livro. A varinha termina em sino (A) ou olho (B); o livro (Escuta) tem olho na capa. Só o nível 150 tem brilho sutil.

ORACLE IDENTITY (bloco fixo, passada de 19/11; vai dentro de TODO prompt de HOMEM e MULHER, antes de "IGNORE" nos níveis 1-60 e depois dele no 61-150; MULHER ganha ainda "FEMININE CUT" (1-60), o coque ("HAIR: ... high BUN") e "Keep her feminine cut." no fim e, onde há volume, "BULK ONLY IN THE ARMOR PIECES, NOT IN HER BODY"): `` ORACLE IDENTITY (never lose this): a battle seer, a warrior-priest of the bells and prophecy who walks into battle, NOT a robed priest, NOT a nun, NOT a generic white wizard. BODY: tall, athletic and broad-shouldered, upright commanding stance, a calm piercing gaze, short neat dark hair and a trimmed beard on the man. SILHOUETTE: an ivory high-collared BATTLE ROBE fitted on the torso and open from the hips into long split panels over fitted dark trousers and tall boots, a night-blue capelet on the shoulders, a wide night-blue sash with a hanging night-blue STOLE embroidered with old-gold BELL and EYE sigils. SIGNATURE DETAIL: a thick BOOK OF PROPHECIES chained to the hip and a pouch of small pale-blue crystals at the belt. CLASS HEADPIECE, always worn: a slim old-gold circlet with a pale-blue crystal at the brow, growing into a tiara at higher levels, hair fully visible. STRONG READABLE COLOR BLOCKS: ivory white #EDE3C8 dominant (45-55%), midnight blue #2A3550 in big solid blocks (25-35%: capelet, sash, stole, linings, trim), old gold #D9B45A as the accent (10-15%: circlet, sigils, bells, trim); strong contrast readable from far away, never a washed-out white blob. Forbidden: bathrobe or kimono look, sandals, old monk or hermit, long loose hair on the shoulders, frail body, pope or cardinal look, glowing parts (only level 150 may have a very subtle inner glow), cartoon look, veil, hood or nun look, pointy wizard hat, plate armor, metal breastplate, helmet, black dominant. ``

MÉTODO (decisão do criador): todo nível, chave ou intermediário, parte do CORPO BASE em pose T (§1b homem, §2 mulher). O prompt diz "IGNORE his/her current clothes and dress him/her in a COMPLETELY NEW level N OUTFIT: ..." e descreve a roupa inteira do zero (torso, ombros, cinto, saia ou roupão, botas, luvas/braçadeiras, materiais). A continuidade vem da paleta e dos temas no texto. VARINHA e LIVRO partem do item do nível anterior do mesmo ramo, mas são redesenhados (cabeça e cabo; capa e fecho).

Modelo de ramos (do 41 ao 150, padrão final): por nível há ITEM (a armadura neutra, sozinha, fundo carvão), HOMEM-A, HOMEM-B, MULHER-A e MULHER-B (o mesmo item interpretado por ramo: Sineiro e Profeta) e uma ARMA única (VARINHA e LIVRO únicos, que fundem sino e olho). Níveis 1 a 37: HOMEM, MULHER, VARINHA e LIVRO completos, partindo do corpo base: "Same Oracle as the input image, ... IGNORE his current clothes and dress him in a COMPLETELY NEW level N outfit. ORACLE IDENTITY: ... OUTFIT: ..." (a MULHER usa "her" e termina com "Keep her feminine cut."). VARINHA e LIVRO são sempre "REDESIGNED for level N". No fim: arte de apresentação (níveis 50 e 150) e os 2 prompts fixos de item.

Regras da cadeia:

  • Todo nível troca peças E materiais: roupão (ou capa), botas e braçadeiras sempre com desenho e material novos; nenhuma peça se repete.
  • Curva de poder: S1 = linho cru; S2 e S3 sóbrios (lã e linho liso, sem bordado nem gema); ornamento só da S4 em diante; capelete de ombros desde a S2 (curto, só nos ombros), capa longa só do 41 em diante (antes, gola pequena, estola e capelete); nunca crânios. O livro das profecias fica acorrentado no quadril desde o piloto (nunca nas mãos na pose T).
  • Braços livres: braçadeiras e mangas podem mudar de forma a cada nível, sempre mais fortes, nunca mais leves.
  • Sem metal pesado; frente e costas com as mesmas peças; nunca mais leve que o nível anterior; traje fosco (só o 150: brilho muito sutil dentro do bordado).

1. Conceito do piloto masculino S1

1a. Conceito (nó "Gerar imagem"; texto no singular, o criador escolhe quantas imagens gerar e fica com 1): `` ONE single full-body character concept of a male fantasy Oracle (battle seer and healer), one figure only, large and centered, high-end action RPG hero concept art in the same painterly 3D style as a rugged barbarian hero, majestic and unforgettable at first sight. A warrior-priest of the bells who walks into battle: tall, athletic and broad-shouldered (NOT frail, NOT skinny, NOT an old monk), upright commanding stance, one hand raised as if reading fate, a calm piercing gaze, short neat dark hair and a trimmed beard. FIXED HEADPIECE: a slim old-gold circlet across the brow with a small pale-blue crystal at the center (it grows into a tiara at higher levels). Level 1 starter gear, simple but already noble: a fitted ivory-white high-collared battle robe that is open from the hips into long split panels over fitted dark trousers and tall soft leather boots (NOT a bathrobe, NOT sandals), a short layered capelet over the shoulders trimmed in midnight blue, a wide midnight-blue sash with a hanging stole embroidered with small old-gold bell and eye sigils, leather bracers on the forearms, a thick leather-bound book of prophecies chained to the hip, a pouch of small pale-blue crystals. He holds a slender wand of pale wood tipped with a raw crystal. Ivory white #EDE3C8 dominant and clean, midnight blue second, old gold as the accent, strong readable contrast. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. FORBIDDEN: bathrobe or kimono look, sandals, old monk or hermit, long loose hair falling on the shoulders, frail body, pope or cardinal look, glowing parts, cartoon look. ``

1b. Pose T do escolhido (entrada = o conceito escolhido; é o CORPO BASE masculino de toda a cadeia): `` Same Oracle as the input image, same face and body, same S1 outfit, symmetric T-pose, arms open, palms down, empty hands, no weapon, front view, full body, the prophecy book stays chained at the hip, matte, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ``

2. Corpo feminino base

Entrada: o piloto masculino em pose T (§1b). Esta imagem é o CORPO BASE feminino. `` Same Oracle as the input image, same S1 pilot outfit design and circlet, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. Make the character a young woman with a REAL FEMININE CUT, not a slimmed-down man with long hair: a feminine face, a defined bust, a clearly narrow waist and rounded hips, toned athletic arms and shoulders, tall, long legs, confident commanding stance. Her version of the outfit: the ivory high-collared battle robe fitted to her bust and waist and open from the hips into long split panels over fitted dark trousers and tall leather boots, the midnight-blue capelet on the shoulders, the wide midnight-blue sash cinching her waist with the stole embroidered with old-gold bell and eye sigils, leather bracers, the book of prophecies chained to the hip, the pouch of pale-blue crystals, the slim old-gold circlet with the pale-blue crystal. HAIR: her dark hair gathered in a high BUN under the circlet, nothing falling on the shoulders. No veil, no bare feet. Elegant and confident, not vulgar. Same palette, matte, no glow. Keep her feminine cut. ``

3. Cabeças

3.1 Prompt da cabeça (entrada = o corpo vestido; trocar o trecho entre colchetes pela descrição de 3.2): `` Head and bare neck only of this character: [DESCRIÇÃO DA CABEÇA]. Same thin gold circlet with a tiny eye on the brow (always worn), hair fully visible, no veil, no hat, stylized fantasy face, not photorealistic, front view, no shoulders, no clothing, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ``

3.2 Descrições (3 + 3, todas distintas; a tiara fica):

  • M1: man in his forties, short dark hair streaked with silver, trimmed beard, calm piercing hazel eyes, strong jaw.
  • M2: man in his thirties, medium curly ash-blond hair with a thin ribbon across the forehead, gentle attentive eyes, light stubble.
  • M3: young man, short dark hair with a straight fringe, pale skin, large listening eyes, faint freckles.
  • F1: woman with straight white-gold hair in a high bun under the circlet, serene commanding face, pale blue eyes.
  • F2: woman in her thirties, braids wound into a halo around the head, warm brown skin, deep brown eyes.
  • F3: young woman, short pageboy cut with a straight fringe, olive skin, wide grey-green eyes.

4. Mãos

`` Male hand pair, both hands only, relaxed open fingers, palms down, same skin tone as the Oracle, a leather bracer edge at each wrist, matte, no ring, no weapon, no book, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ` ` Female hand pair, both hands only, slender relaxed open fingers, palms down, same skin tone as the female Oracle, a leather bracer edge at each wrist, matte, no ring, no weapon, no book, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ``

5. Cadeia de níveis (1 ao 150)

Entradas: HOMEM = corpo base masculino em pose T (§1b); MULHER = corpo base feminino em pose T (§2); VARINHA e LIVRO = o item do nível anterior (nível 1: os do conceito escolhido), sempre REDESIGNED. Ordem: 1, 6, 11, 15, 21, 26, 31, 37 (HOMEM, MULHER, VARINHA, LIVRO completos); do 41 ao 150, modelo de ramos por nível: ITEM neutro, HOMEM-A, HOMEM-B, MULHER-A, MULHER-B e uma VARINHA e um LIVRO únicos.

Nível 1 · S1 · Linho Cru (piloto)

Parte de: corpo base. HOMEM `` Same Oracle as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ORACLE IDENTITY (never lose this): a battle seer, a warrior-priest of the bells and prophecy who walks into battle, NOT a robed priest, NOT a nun, NOT a generic white wizard. NEW spine and clasp: a simple rope tie. Closed, front view, plain light grey background, no hands, no glow. ``

Nível 11 · S2 · Lã Creme (sóbrio)

Parte de: corpo base. HOMEM `` Same Oracle as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. ORACLE IDENTITY (never lose this): a battle seer, a warrior-priest of the bells and prophecy who walks into battle, NOT a robed priest, NOT a nun, NOT a generic white wizard. BODY: tall, athletic and broad-shouldered, upright commanding stance, a calm piercing gaze, short neat dark hair and a trimmed beard on the man. SILHOUETTE: an ivory high-collared BATTLE ROBE fitted on the torso and open from the hips into long split panels over fitted dark trousers and tall boots, a night-blue capelet on the shoulders, a wide night-blue sash with a hanging night-blue STOLE embroidered with old-gold BELL and EYE sigils. CLASS HEADPIECE, always worn: a slim old-gold circlet with a pale-blue crystal at the brow, growing into a tiara at higher levels, hair fully visible. STRONG READABLE COLOR BLOCKS: ivory white #EDE3C8 dominant (45-55%), midnight blue #2A3550 in big solid blocks (25-35%: capelet, sash, stole, linings, trim), old gold #D9B45A as the accent (10-15%: circlet, sigils, bells, trim); strong contrast readable from far away, never a washed-out white blob. Forbidden: bathrobe or kimono look, sandals, old monk or hermit, long loose hair on the shoulders, frail body, pope or cardinal look, glowing parts (only level 150 may have a very subtle inner glow), cartoon look, veil, hood or nun look, pointy wizard hat, plate armor, metal breastplate, helmet, black dominant. IGNORE her current clothes and dress her in a COMPLETELY NEW level 44 outfit, the Sineiro interpretation of the level 44 item. OUTFIT: a split cape in two long DEEP NIGHT-BLUE panels (blue, not ivory) open at the front with gold bell medallions, a wide gold-embroidered shoulder yoke, a deep ivory bell-hem robe, a long ivory stole with night-blue script, a night-blue sash with the chained book of prophecies and a cluster of name ribbons each ending in a tiny bell, wide bracers shaped as bell medallions, boots with bell straps and curled tops, a TALL BELL-TOWER crown: stacked gold tiers with small bells hanging between its points above the open eye. No heavy metal, no veil, matte, no glow. FEMININE CUT: a fitted bodice shaped to her bust, a wide sash cinching a clearly narrow waist, the battle-robe panels opening from her rounded hips, a shaped neckline, graceful volume in the sleeves and capelet; a real female figure, never a slimmed-down man with long hair. BULK ONLY IN THE ARMOR PIECES, NOT IN HER BODY: the volume lives in the cloth pieces (layered robe tiers, wide sleeves, full capelet or cape, heavy stole), while her body stays slender with a narrow cinched waist and feminine hips; never boxy, never masculine. CRYSTAL TOUCH (incredibly subtle): only one or two tiny matte pale-blue crystal flecks embedded somewhere in the gear, almost unnoticeable at first glance, no glow. HAIR: her dark hair gathered in a high BUN under the circlet, nothing falling on the shoulders. Keep her feminine cut. ``

CLASS HEADPIECE, always worn: a slim old-gold circlet with a pale-blue crystal at the brow, growing into a tiara at higher levels, hair fully visible. STRONG READABLE COLOR BLOCKS: ivory white #EDE3C8 dominant (45-55%), midnight blue #2A3550 in big solid blocks (25-35%: capelet, sash, stole, linings, trim), old gold #D9B45A as the accent (10-15%: circlet, sigils, bells, trim); strong contrast readable from far away, never a washed-out white blob. Forbidden: bathrobe or kimono look, sandals, old monk or hermit, long loose hair on the shoulders, frail body, pope or cardinal look, glowing parts (only level 150 may have a very subtle inner glow), cartoon look, veil, hood or nun look, pointy wizard hat, plate armor, metal breastplate, helmet, black dominant. IGNORE her current clothes and dress her in a COMPLETELY NEW level 51 outfit, the Sineiro interpretation of the level 51 item. OUTFIT: a three-tier layered cape of three overlapping bell shapes to the ground in pale-gold silk lined in deep night blue, a shoulder collar of small gold bells on cloth, wide gold filigree borders, a high back collar fan, an ivory bell-hem robe, a long night-blue stole with gold script, a filigree belt with the chained book of prophecies and gold name tablets, wide filigree bracers, tall boots with filigree toe caps, a crown ring of bells around the open eye. No heavy metal, no veil, matte, no glow. FEMININE CUT: a fitted bodice shaped to her bust, a wide sash cinching a clearly narrow waist, the battle-robe panels opening from her rounded hips, a shaped neckline, graceful volume in the sleeves and capelet; a real female figure, never a slimmed-down man with long hair. BULK ONLY IN THE ARMOR PIECES, NOT IN HER BODY: the volume lives in the cloth pieces (layered robe tiers, wide sleeves, full capelet or cape, heavy stole), while her body stays slender with a narrow cinched waist and feminine hips; never boxy, never masculine. CRYSTAL TOUCH (incredibly subtle): only one or two tiny matte pale-blue crystal flecks embedded somewhere in the gear, almost unnoticeable at first glance, no glow. HAIR: her dark hair gathered in a high BUN under the circlet, nothing falling on the shoulders. Keep her feminine cut. ``

OUTFIT: (Living Bell form) a cape whose hem is set with crystal bell points and a large halo ring of cloth-mounted cut crystal bells behind the head, a cream robe, a spire belt with the chained book of prophecies, crystal-spire bracers, boots with crystal bell cuffs, crystal bell spires crowning the tiara. No heavy metal, no veil, matte, no glow. SHOWPIECE: this is a legendary milestone armor (form level), the most imposing and epic look of this class so far, an instant 'wow' at first sight: a bigger, more dramatic silhouette than any earlier level, rich hand-crafted detail everywhere, vivid accents in the class colors, still matte, no glow. CRYSTAL TOUCH (subtle): a few small discreet matte pale-blue crystal inlays on two or three pieces, a quiet accent only, never dominant, no glow. HAIR: her dark hair gathered in a high BUN under the circlet, nothing falling on the shoulders. Keep her feminine cut. ``

6. Arte de apresentação (Oráculo: nível 50 e nível 150)

Duas versões de arte de capa e retrato, uma no nível 50 e outra no nível 150. Entrada: as imagens aprovadas de HOMEM e MULHER do nível indicado, do mesmo ramo (se quiser as duas variantes, repetir com o outro ramo). O nível 50 usa o traje do nível 48, o último vestido ao chegar nos 50; o nível 150 usa o 150-A ou o 150-B. Anexar também a arma aprovada do nível.

Versão nível 50

KEY ART (anexar HOMEM e MULHER juntos) `` Epic key art for the Oracle class of a dark fantasy RPG: the two attached characters (male and female) together, same faces, bodies and outfits, holding their small wands tipped with a bell and an eye and closed books with an eye on the cover in powerful poses, a windswept stone terrace of a bell tower at dusk with great bronze bells, lantern light and the city far below, dramatic rim light, painterly game cover style, high detail, wide 16:9 composition, no text. ` RETRATO (um por personagem: HOMEM e MULHER, um de cada vez) ` Portrait bust of the attached character: same face, hair and outfit. Three-quarter view, confident look, dramatic rim light, dark charcoal background, painterly game portrait style, high detail, square 1:1, no text. ``

Versão nível 150

KEY ART (anexar HOMEM e MULHER juntos) `` Epic key art for the Oracle class of a dark fantasy RPG: the two attached characters (male and female) together, same faces, bodies and outfits, holding their prestige wands with a great bell and a great eye, and books with gold filigree covers in powerful poses, the same bell-tower terrace at night under a vast sky, a huge ring of hanging bells and a great carved eye above them, drifting threads of warm light, dramatic rim light, painterly game cover style, high detail, wide 16:9 composition, no text. ` RETRATO (um por personagem: HOMEM e MULHER, um de cada vez) ` Portrait bust of the attached character: same face, hair and outfit. 7. Prompts FIXOS de arte do item (fundo carvão)

Entrada: a imagem do traje aprovado (para ARMADURA) ou da arma aprovada (para ARMA; vale também para o livro, o escudo e a lança). Os mesmos dois prompts valem para todo nível; nunca editar.

ARMADURA: `` The full torso armor from the input image displayed alone as a game item, including ALL its parts: collar or cape, both pauldrons, chest pieces and straps, belt and the full skirt. No boots, no bracers, no gloves, no arms, no body. Front view, centered, plain dark charcoal background, soft studio light from the top-left, subtle rim light, high detail. ` ARMA: ` The same weapon from the input image displayed alone as a game item: identical design, materials and colors. Diagonal angle from bottom-left to top-right, centered, plain dark charcoal background, soft studio light from the top-left, subtle rim light, high detail, no hands, no text. ``

Ragna Tale é um nome de trabalho; pode mudar antes do lançamento.
Jogo em desenvolvimento. Imagens são conceitos de design, não capturas do jogo final. Sem cookies e sem rastreadores.
